diff --git a/env2json.py b/env2json.py
@@ -0,0 +1,40 @@
+import os
+import re
+import sys
+import json
+
+DEFAULT_ENV_FILE = ".env.local"
+env_vars = []
+
+if len(sys.argv) > 1:
+    ENV_FILE = sys.argv[1]
+else:
+    ENV_FILE = DEFAULT_ENV_FILE
+
+try:
+    with open(ENV_FILE, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
+        content = f.read()
+except FileNotFoundError:
+    print(f"ã‚¨ãƒ©ãƒ¼: ãƒ•ã‚¡ã‚¤ãƒ« '{ENV_FILE}' ãŒè¦‹ã¤ã‹ã‚Šã¾ã›ã‚“ã€‚")
+    print("ä½¿ç”¨æ–¹æ³•: python convert.py [envãƒ•ã‚¡ã‚¤ãƒ«ã®ãƒ‘ã‚¹]")
+    sys.exit(1)
+
+for line in content.splitlines():
+    line = line.strip()
+    if not line or line.startswith('#'):
+        continue
+
+    match = re.match(r'^\s*([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)\s*=\s*(.*)\s*$', line)
+    if match:
+        key = match.group(1)
+        value = match.group(2).strip()
+        
+        if value.startswith(('"', "'")) and value.endswith((value[0])):
+            value = value[1:-1]
+            
+        env_vars.append({
+            "key": key,
+            "value": value
+        })
+
+print(json.dumps(env_vars, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False))

+import os
+import subprocess
+
+def convert_mpeg_to_mp4():
+    input_dir = r"E:\Pictures\ä»–äººä½œå“\ãƒ›ãƒ­\sukumiz"
+    extension = ".mpeg"
+
+    files = [f for f in os.listdir(input_dir) if f.lower().endswith(extension)]
+
+    for file in files:
+        input_path = os.path.join(input_dir, file)
+        output_path = os.path.join(input_dir, os.path.splitext(file)[0] + ".mp4")
+
+        command = [
+            "ffmpeg",
+            "-i", input_path,
+            "-c:v", "hevc_nvenc",
+            output_path
+        ]
+
+        try:
+            subprocess.run(command, check=True)
+        except subprocess.CalledProcessError as e:
+            print(f"Error converting {file}: {e}")
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    convert_mpeg_to_mp4()
